([princemanjee/EngineeredPromptLibrary](https://github.com/princemanjee/EngineeredPromptLibrary/tree/HEAD/plugins/pl-terminals/skills/dax-terminal) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **dax-time-intelligence**: Generate complete time intelligence measure sets for Power BI semantic models. ([Insightpulseai/powerbi-skills](https://github.com/Insightpulseai/powerbi-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/dax-time-intelligence))
- **dax-udf**: Reference for DAX User-Defined Functions (UDFs) in Power BI semantic models. Use this when building reusable function definitions with type safety and parameter modes (Val/Expr/AnyRef). Always consult before writing repeated DAX logic that could be abstracted into a single function — UDFs eliminate boilerplate and make complex patterns like time intelligence, statistical functions, and table filters reusable across the entire model. ([jonathan-pap/PowerBI-Claude-Design](https://github.com/jonathan-pap/PowerBI-Claude-Design/tree/HEAD/dax-udf))
- **dax-udf-authoring**: Use when writing your own DAX user-defined function (FUNCTION) — parameter types (AnyRef, ColumnRef, MeasureRef, CalendarRef), VAL vs EXPR, TABLEOF/NAMEOF, optional parameters, naming, and known parser bugs. For existing UDFs use dax-lib; for built-ins, dax-reference. Triggers on "write a DAX UDF", "define a DAX FUNCTION", "ANYREF", "VAL vs EXPR", "TABLEOF", "optional parameter in a UDF". ([CSalcedoDataBI/dax-for-agents](https://github.com/CSalcedoDataBI/dax-for-agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/dax-for-agents/skills/dax-udf-authoring) · ★3)
- **dax-window-functions**: Use when writing a DAX measure with WINDOW, OFFSET, INDEX, RANK, ROWNUMBER, MOVINGAVERAGE or RUNNINGSUM (ORDERBY/PARTITIONBY/MATCHBY) — rolling averages, running totals, prior-period rows, ranking within a partition. Covers ABS vs REL and the ALLSELECTED default that silently returns blanks. Triggers on "rolling average", "running total", "trailing N", "OFFSET returns blank", "ABS vs REL", "PARTITIONBY". ([CSalcedoDataBI/dax-for-agents](https://github.com/CSalcedoDataBI/dax-for-agents/tree/HEAD/plugins/dax-for-agents/skills/dax-window-functions) · ★3)

- **day-close**: Day Close protocol. Alias for /run-protocol close day — symmetric with /day-open. ([iwesys/IWE](https://github.com/iwesys/IWE/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/day-close))
- **day-close**: 業務終了時に、その日触ったLinearイシューへ進捗コメントを投稿し、ステータスとdueDateを整理するスキル。当日のSlack発言とLinearのメタデータから進捗の下書きを自動生成し、まとめて確認を取ってから書き込む。「作業終わり」「進捗載せたい」「今日の進捗」「進捗記載」「作業を終える」などのキーワードで発動する。対になるスキルは day-open。 ([ryuya-matsunawa/dotfiles](https://github.com/ryuya-matsunawa/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/agents/.agents/skills/synced/4ed756b4-b50a-48be-ba95-e42daab96982_81a7a9b3-9869-4c39-b796-f3cc11087eef/day-close))
- **day-close**: Close the owner's working day. Use when the owner says — English: “close the day”, “Close the day.”, “shutdown”, “wrap up”, “wrap up today”, or that they are done for today; Italian: «chiudiamo la giornata», «chiudi la giornata», «fine giornata», «per oggi ho finito». The board, the waits and the machinery are read first; two questions; decisions, the journal line, tasks and waits filed, tasks closed and waits lifted through the door on the owner's yes only; nothing said today left unfiled without asking; tomorrow proposed; then the push stops. Also answers to its former name, “close the day”. ([stefanoritteri-code/workspace-skills](https://github.com/stefanoritteri-code/workspace-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/workspace/skills/day-close))
- **day-close**: Протокол закрытия дня (Day Close). Алиас для /run-protocol close day -- симметрия с /day-open. ([TserenTserenov/iwe-server-config](https://github.com/TserenTserenov/iwe-server-config/tree/HEAD/server-extensions/claude-skills/day-close/day-close))
- **day-close-audit**: Debris-and-hygiene check for TheIgors, run during /day-close. MANDATORY part of day-close — never skip. Checks for debris (temp files, leaked runtime state, dead code), tests, file placement, code smells, registry coherence, inertia check, thread hygiene, log sizes, OR burn rate, DB schema, duplication, habit health, TWM coverage, dependency hygiene, credential scan, and simplification review. Fix small issues now, ticket anything bigger. ([akienm/TheIgors](https://github.com/akienm/TheIgors/tree/HEAD/lab/claudecode/cc_skills/day-close-audit))
- **day-close-audit**: Debris-and-hygiene check for UU, run during /day-close. MANDATORY part of day-close — never skip. Checks for debris (temp files, leaked runtime state, dead code), tests, file placement, code smells, registry coherence, inertia check, thread hygiene, log sizes, OR burn rate, DB schema, duplication, habit health, TWM coverage, dependency hygiene, credential scan, and simplification review. - **day-copy**: Пишет и проверяет тексты на экранах Day на русском и казахском — кабинет хозяина, Mini App, гостевые страницы (/s/, /o/, /g/, /c/), сообщения гостям и горничной. Единый словарь Day, обращение на «вы», тон по ситуации, склонения, деньги и даты. Применять при любой правке строк в locales, в компонентах, в макетах public/mock и в текстах бота. ([Mykty-Ayan/day](https://github.com/Mykty-Ayan/day/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/day-copy))
- **day-counter**: One-prompt animation style from the CampusAI studio. Makes a count of days between two real dates on a rolling odometer, with the dates that matter flagged along a day strip. ([CampusAI-app/campusai-studio](https://github.com/CampusAI-app/campusai-studio/tree/HEAD/companion-skills/day-counter))
- **day-cp**: ClarityPledge half of the daily run — prod smoke, git/spec health, Supabase + Sentry + Mixpanel user intelligence, repo baseline, RLS and function-grant drift, reflection, goals and branches. Records a status per check, findings and people for the day report. ([slavochek2/claritypledge](https://github.com/slavochek2/claritypledge/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/day-cp) · ★1)
- **day-cycle**: /day-cycle — оркестрация полного цикла дня адвента от задания владельца до прода. Ведёт фазы по ролям с моделями по ярусам, гейтами по классу изменения и критерием выхода из каждой фазы. Вызывать при старте нового дня или крупной задачи. ([MikeKharr/ai-advent-2026](https://github.com/MikeKharr/ai-advent-2026/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/day-cycle))
- **day-dashboard**: Use when Jordan asks to see, refresh, open, or set up his day dashboard / day brief / "what's on today" page, or invokes /day. It regenerates the private hourly dashboard (calendar, email, meeting-note action items, Slack, Linear, Confluence) and points at the served page. A plain question about his schedule is not by itself a request for the dashboard — answer that directly.
